<path>/source/head/.cursor/rules/karpathy-guidelines.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code to avoid overcomplication, make surgical changes, surface assumptions, and define verifiable success criteria.
3: alwaysApply: true
4: ---
5: 
6: # Karpathy behavioral guidelines
7: 
8: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.
9: 
10: **Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.
11: 
12: ## 1. Think Before Coding
13: 
14: **Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**
15: 
16: Before implementing:
17: 
18: - State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.
19: - If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.
20: - If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.
21: - If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.
22: 
23: ## 2. Simplicity First
24: 
25: **Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**
26: 
27: - No features beyond what was asked.
28: - No abstractions for single-use code.
29: - No "flexibility" or "configurability" that wasn't requested.
30: - No error handling for impossible scenarios.
31: - If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.
32: 
33: Ask yourself: "Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?" If yes, simplify.
34: 
35: ## 3. Surgical Changes
36: 
37: **Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**
38: 
39: When editing existing code:
40: 
41: - Don't "improve" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.
42: - Don't refactor things that aren't broken.
43: - Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.
44: - If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.
45: 
46: When your changes create orphans:
47: 
48: - Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.
49: - Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.
50: 
51: The test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.
52: 
53: ## 4. Goal-Driven Execution
54: 
55: **Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**
56: 
57: Transform tasks into verifiable goals:
58: 
59: - "Add validation" → "Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass"
60: - "Fix the bug" → "Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass"
61: - "Refactor X" → "Ensure tests pass before and after"
62: 
63: For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:
64: 
65: ```
66: 1. [Step] → verify: [check]
67: 2. [Step] → verify: [check]
68: 3. [Step] → verify: [check]
69: ```
70: 
71: Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria ("make it work") require constant clarification.
72: 
73: ---
74: 
75: **These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.

(End of file - total 75 lines)
</content>

Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**\n\nBefore implementing:\n\n- State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.\n- If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.\n- If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.\n- If something is unclear, stop. Nothing speculative.**\n\n- No features beyond what was asked.\n- No abstractions for single-use code.\n- No \"flexibility\" or \"configurability\" that wasn't requested.\n- No error handling for impossible scenarios.\n- If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.\n\nAsk yourself: \"Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?\" If yes, simplify.\n\n## 3. <type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode. Always pick the simplest solution that works.
3: globs:
4: alwaysApply: true
5: ---
6: 
7: # Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode
8: 
9: You are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.
10: 
11: Before writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:
12: 
13: 1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)
14: 2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.
15: 3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.
16: 4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.
17: 5. Does an already-installed dependency solve it? Use it.
18: 6. Can this be one line? Make it one line.
19: 7. Only then: write the minimum code that works.
20: 
21: The ladder runs after you understand the problem, not instead of it: read the task and the code it touches, trace the real flow end to end, then climb.
22: 
23: Bug fix = root cause, not symptom: a report names a symptom. Grep every caller of the function you touch and fix the shared function once — one guard there is a smaller diff than one per caller, and patching only the path the ticket names leaves a sibling caller still broken.
24: 
25: Rules:
26: 
27: - No abstractions that weren't explicitly requested.
28: - No new dependency if it can be avoided.
29: - No boilerplate nobody asked for.
30: - Deletion over addition. Boring over clever. Fewest files possible.
31: - Shortest working diff wins, but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.
32: - Question complex requests: "Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?"
33: - Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.
34: - Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.
35: 
36: Not lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.

(End of file - total 36 lines)
</content>

169: +// logicalNodeContainers returns a single logical topology node.
170: +func logicalNodeContainers(
171: +	containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer,
172: +) []clabruntime.GenericContainer {
173: +	// Node names are unique only within a lab.
174: +	type nodeKey struct{ lab, node string }
175: +
176: +	holders := make(map[nodeKey]*clabruntime.GenericContainer)
177: +	hasCPMA := make(map[nodeKey]bool)
178: +
179: +	for idx := range containers {
180: +		l := containers[idx].Labels
181: +		key := nodeKey{l[clabconstants.Containerlab], l[clabconstants.RootNodeName]}
182: +
183: +		switch {
184: +		case key.node == "":
185: +		case l[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true":
186: +			holders[key] = &containers[idx]
187: +		case l[clabconstants.NodeName] == key.node+"-a":
188: +			hasCPMA[key] = true
189:  		}
190:  	}

195: -			return c.Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true"
196: -		})
197: +	result := make([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, 0, len(containers))
198: +
199: +	for idx := range containers {
200: +		ctr := containers[idx]
201: +		name := ctr.Labels[clabconstants.NodeName]
202: +		key := nodeKey{
203: +			ctr.Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab],
204: +			ctr.Labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName],
205: +		}
206: +
207: +		// Components are named <root node>-<lowercase slot>.
208: +		isCPM := name == key.node+"-a" || (name == key.node+"-b" && !hasCPMA[key])
209: +		if ctr.Labels[clabconstants.ToolType] != "" ||
210: +			ctr.Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true" || (key.node != "" && !isCPM) {
211: +			continue
212: +		}
213: +
214: +		if key.node == "" {
215: +			key.node = name
216: +		}
217: +
218: +		if holder := holders[key]; holder != nil {
219: +			ctr.NetworkName = holder.NetworkName
220: +			ctr.NetworkSettings = holder.NetworkSettings
221: +			ctr.Ports = holder.Ports
222: +		}

342: index 2fe51f6..2a5b391 100644
343: --- a/docs/cmd/inspect/index.md
344: +++ b/docs/cmd/inspect/index.md
345: @@ -65,10 +65,20 @@ The data is provided in the context of a container that belongs to a containerla
346:  
347:  The `inspect` command produces a brief summary about the running lab components. It is also possible to get a full view on the running containers by adding `--details` flag.
348:  
349:  With this flag inspect command will output every bit of information about the running containers. This is what `docker inspect` command provides.
350:  
351: +#### containers
352: +
353: +By default `inspect` shows one entry per topology node.
354: +
355: +Nodes that consist of several containers (ie. distributed SR-SIM) are represented by a single node that carries the management addresses of the node.
356: +
357: +The local flag `--containers` will list every container of a lab rather than just the logical nodes.
358: +
359: +When the lab is selected with the `--topo` flag, only the containers of the nodes defined in that topology file are listed.
360: +
361:  #### wide
362:  
363:  The local `-w | --wide` flag adds all available columns to the `inspect` output table.
364:  
365:  ### Examples
366: diff --git a/docs/manual/kinds/srl.md b/docs/manual/kinds/srl.md
367: index 269d4ba..3aac436 100644
368: --- a/docs/manual/kinds/srl.md
369: +++ b/docs/manual/kinds/srl.md
370: @@ -468,11 +468,11 @@ The license file lifts these limitations as well as unlocks chassis-based platfo
371:  
372:  ### Reboot
373:  
374:  A reboot issued from within the node, for example with `tools platform chassis reboot`, restarts the SR Linux container. The node comes back with its links, saved configuration and management address intact.
375:  
376: -To support that, SR Linux nodes default to the `unless-stopped` [restart policy](../nodes.md#restart-policy), and containerlab keeps the links and the management address of each node in an internal `<node-name>-netns` container. The internal container is only listed by `containerlab inspect --all`, and it is removed together with the node.
377: +To support that, SR Linux nodes default to the `unless-stopped` [restart policy](../nodes.md#restart-policy), and containerlab keeps the links and the management address of each node in an internal `<node-name>-netns` container. The internal container is only listed by `containerlab inspect` with the [`--containers`](../../cmd/inspect/index.md#containers) or `--details` flag, and it is removed together with the node.
378:  
379:  Use `containerlab stop`, `start` and `restart` to manage the lifecycle of SR Linux nodes instead of restarting the containers with the container runtime directly.
380:  
381:  Nodes with a user-defined [`network-mode`](../nodes.md#network-mode) do not use the internal container.

111: func inspectFn(cobraCmd *cobra.Command, o *Options) error {
112: 	if o.Global.TopologyName == "" && o.Global.TopologyFile == "" && !o.Destroy.All {
113: 		return fmt.Errorf(
114: 			"provide either a lab name (--name) or a topology file path (--topo) or the --all flag",
115: 		)
116: 	}
117: 
118: 	// Format validation (only relevant if --details is NOT used)
119: 	if !o.Inspect.Details &&
120: 		o.Inspect.Format != clabconstants.FormatTable &&
121: 		o.Inspect.Format != clabconstants.FormatJSON &&
122: 		o.Inspect.Format != clabconstants.FormatCSV {
123: 		return fmt.Errorf(
124: 			"output format %q is not supported when --details is not used, use "+
125: 				"'table', 'json' or 'csv'",
126: 			o.Inspect.Format,
127: 		)
128: 	}
129: 	// If --details is used, the format is implicitly JSON.
130: 	if o.Inspect.Details {
131: 		o.Inspect.Format = clabconstants.FormatJSON // Force JSON format if details are requested
132: 	}
133: 
134: 	c, err := clabcore.NewContainerLab(o.ToClabOptions()...)
135: 	if err != nil {
136: 		return err
137: 	}
138: 
139: 	containers, err := listContainers(cobraCmd.Context(), c, o)
140: 	if err != nil {
141: 		return err
142: 	}
143: 
144: 	// Handle empty results
145: 	if len(containers) == 0 {
146: 		switch o.Inspect.Format {
147: 		case clabconstants.FormatJSON:
148: 			fmt.Println("{}")
149: 		case clabconstants.FormatCSV:
150: 			fmt.Println(
151: 				"lab_name,labPath,absLabPath,name,container_id,image,kind,state,status," +
152: 					"ipv4_address,ipv6_address,owner",
153: 			)
154: 		default: // Table format
155: 			log.Info("no containers found")
156: 		}
157: 
158: 		return err
159: 	}
160: 
161: 	// Handle --details (always produces grouped JSON output)
162: 	if o.Inspect.Details {
163: 		return printContainerDetailsJSON(containers)
164: 	}
165: 
166: 	// Handle non-details cases (table or grouped JSON summary)
167: 	err = PrintContainerInspect(containers, o)
168: 
169: 	return err
170: }

172: // listContainers handles listing containers based on different criteria (topology or labels).
173: func listContainers(
174: 	ctx context.Context,
175: 	c *clabcore.CLab,
176: 	o *Options,
177: ) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error) {
178: 	if c.LabRuntime != nil {
179: 		return c.ListLabRuntimeContainers(ctx, o.Destroy.All)
180: 	}
181: 
182: 	var listOptions []clabcore.ListOption
183: 
184: 	switch {
185: 	case o.Global.TopologyFile != "":
186: 		listOptions = append(listOptions, clabcore.WithListLabName(c.Config.Name))
187: 	case o.Global.TopologyName != "":
188: 		listOptions = append(listOptions, clabcore.WithListLabName(o.Global.TopologyName))
189: 	default:
190: 		listOptions = append(listOptions, clabcore.WithListclabLabelExists())
191: 	}
192: 
193: 	containers, err := c.ListContainers(ctx, listOptions...)
194: 	if err != nil {
195: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list containers based on labels: %s", err)
196: 	}
197: 
198: 	if o.Global.TopologyFile != "" {
199: 		containers = slices.DeleteFunc(containers, func(ctr clabruntime.GenericContainer) bool {
200: 			_, isNode := c.Nodes[ctr.Labels[clabconstants.NodeName]]
201: 			_, isComponent := c.Nodes[ctr.Labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName]]
202: 
203: 			return !isNode && !isComponent
204: 		})
205: 
206: 		// containers that containerlab did not create (ie. ext-container) have no lab labels.
207: 		nodeContainers, err := c.ListNodesContainersIgnoreNotFound(ctx)
208: 		if err != nil {
209: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list containers based on topology: %s", err)
210: 		}
211: 
212: 		for idx := range nodeContainers {
213: 			if !slices.ContainsFunc(containers, func(ctr clabruntime.GenericContainer) bool {
214: 				return ctr.ID == nodeContainers[idx].ID
215: 			}) {
216: 				containers = append(containers, nodeContainers[idx])
217: 			}
218: 		}
219: 	}
220: 
221: 	// --details always describes every container of a lab.
222: 	if o.Inspect.Containers || o.Inspect.Details {
223: 		return containers, nil
224: 	}
225: 
226: 	return logicalNodeContainers(containers), nil
227: }
228: 
229: // logicalNodeContainers returns a single logical topology node.
230: func logicalNodeContainers(
231: 	containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer,
232: ) []clabruntime.GenericContainer {
233: 	// Node names are unique only within a lab.
234: 	type nodeKey struct{ lab, node string }
235: 
236: 	holders := make(map[nodeKey]*clabruntime.GenericContainer)
237: 	hasCPMA := make(map[nodeKey]bool)
238: 
239: 	for idx := range containers {
240: 		l := containers[idx].Labels
241: 		key := nodeKey{l[clabconstants.Containerlab], l[clabconstants.RootNodeName]}
242: 
243: 		switch {
244: 		case key.node == "":
245: 		case l[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true":
246: 			holders[key] = &containers[idx]
247: 		case l[clabconstants.NodeName] == key.node+"-a":
248: 			hasCPMA[key] = true
249: 		}
250: 	}
251: 
252: 	result := make([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, 0, len(containers))
253: 
254: 	for idx := range containers {
255: 		ctr := containers[idx]
256: 		name := ctr.Labels[clabconstants.NodeName]
257: 		key := nodeKey{
258: 			ctr.Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab],
259: 			ctr.Labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName],
260: 		}
261: 
262: 		// Components are named <root node>-<lowercase slot>.
263: 		isCPM := name == key.node+"-a" || (name == key.node+"-b" && !hasCPMA[key])
264: 		if ctr.Labels[clabconstants.ToolType] != "" ||
265: 			ctr.Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true" || (key.node != "" && !isCPM) {
266: 			continue
267: 		}
268: 
269: 		if key.node == "" {
270: 			key.node = name
271: 		}
272: 
273: 		if holder := holders[key]; holder != nil {
274: 			ctr.NetworkName = holder.NetworkName
275: 			ctr.NetworkSettings = holder.NetworkSettings
276: 			ctr.Ports = holder.Ports
277: 		}
278: 
279: 		result = append(result, ctr)
280: 	}
281: 
282: 	return result
283: }
284: 
285: func toTableData(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails, o *Options) []tableWriter.Row {
286: 	tabData := make([]tableWriter.Row, 0, len(contDetails))
287: 
288: 	for i := range contDetails {
289: 		d := &contDetails[i]
290: 		tabRow := tableWriter.Row{}
291: 
292: 		if o.Destroy.All {
293: 			tabRow = append(tabRow, d.LabPath, d.LabName)
294: 		}
295: 
296: 		if o.Inspect.Wide {
297: 			tabRow = append(tabRow, d.Owner)
298: 		}
299: 
300: 		// we do not want to print status other than health in the table view
301: 		if !strings.Contains(d.Status, "health") {
302: 			d.Status = ""
303: 		} else {
304: 			d.Status = fmt.Sprintf("(%s)", d.Status)
305: 		}
306: 
307: 		// Common fields
308: 		if o.Inspect.Wide {
309: 			// Print all fields on one line, no newlines
310: 			tabRow = append(tabRow,
311: 				d.Name,
312: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", d.Kind, d.Image),
313: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", d.State, d.Status),
314: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s %s",
315: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv4Address),
316: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv6Address)))
317: 		} else {
318: 			tabRow = append(tabRow,
319: 				d.Name,
320: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s\n%s", d.Kind, d.Image),
321: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s\n%s", d.State, d.Status),
322: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s\n%s",
323: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv4Address),
324: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv6Address)))
325: 		}
326: 
327: 		tabData = append(tabData, tabRow)
328: 	}
329: 
330: 	return tabData
331: }

333: // getShortestTopologyPath calculates the relative path to the provided topology file from
334: // the current working directory and returns it if it is shorted than the absolute path p.
335: func getShortestTopologyPath(p string) (string, error) {
336: 	if p == "" {
337: 		return "", nil
338: 	}
339: 	if strings.Contains(p, "://") {
340: 		return p, nil
341: 	}
342: 
343: 	// get topo file path relative of the cwd
344: 	cwd, err := os.Getwd()
345: 	if err != nil {
346: 		return "", err
347: 	}
348: 
349: 	relPath, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, p)
350: 	if err != nil {
351: 		return "", err
352: 	}
353: 
354: 	if len(relPath) < len(p) {
355: 		return relPath, nil
356: 	}
357: 
358: 	return p, nil
359: }
360: 
361: // printContainerDetailsJSON handles the detailed JSON output of containers grouped by lab name.
362: func printContainerDetailsJSON(containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer) error {
363: 	groupedDetails := make(map[string][]clabruntime.GenericContainer)
364: 	// Sort containers first by lab name, then by container name for consistent output
365: 	sort.Slice(containers, func(i, j int) bool {
366: 		labNameI := containers[i].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]
367: 		labNameJ := containers[j].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]
368: 
369: 		if labNameI == labNameJ {
370: 			// Use the first name if available
371: 			nameI := ""
372: 			if len(containers[i].Names) > 0 {
373: 				nameI = containers[i].Names[0]
374: 			}
375: 
376: 			nameJ := ""
377: 			if len(containers[j].Names) > 0 {
378: 				nameJ = containers[j].Names[0]
379: 			}
380: 
381: 			return nameI < nameJ
382: 		}
383: 
384: 		return labNameI < labNameJ
385: 	})
386: 
387: 	// Group the sorted containers
388: 	for idx := range containers {
389: 		labName := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]
390: 		// Ensure labName exists, default to a placeholder if missing
391: 		// (shouldn't happen with filters)
392: 		if labName == "" {
393: 			labName = "_unknown_lab_"
394: 		}
395: 
396: 		groupedDetails[labName] = append(groupedDetails[labName], containers[idx])
397: 	}
398: 
399: 	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(groupedDetails, "", "  ")
400: 	if err != nil {
401: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal grouped container details: %v", err)
402: 	}
403: 
404: 	fmt.Println(string(b))
405: 
406: 	return nil
407: }
408: 
409: func printContainerInspectJSON(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails) error {
410: 	// Group summary results by LabName
411: 	// Use a map where keys are lab names and values are slices of container details
412: 	groupedLabs := make(map[string][]clabtypes.ContainerDetails)
413: 
414: 	for idx := range contDetails {
415: 		labName := contDetails[idx].LabName
416: 		if labName == "" {
417: 			labName = "_unknown_lab_" // Should not happen if filters work correctly
418: 		}
419: 		// Assign the *entire* ContainerDetails struct (including AbsLabPath)
420: 		groupedLabs[labName] = append(groupedLabs[labName], contDetails[idx])
421: 	}
422: 
423: 	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(groupedLabs, "", "  ")
424: 	if err != nil {
425: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal grouped container summary: %v", err)
426: 	}
427: 
428: 	fmt.Println(string(b))
429: 
430: 	return nil
431: }

433: func printContainerInspectTable(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails, o *Options) {
434: 	// Generate and render table using the summary data (which uses relative LabPath)
435: 	tabData := toTableData(contDetails, o)
436: 	table := tableWriter.NewWriter()
437: 	table.SetOutputMirror(os.Stdout)
438: 	table.SetStyle(tableWriter.StyleRounded)
439: 	table.Style().Format.Header = text.FormatTitle
440: 	table.Style().Format.HeaderAlign = text.AlignCenter
441: 	table.Style().Options.SeparateRows = true
442: 	table.Style().Color = tableWriter.ColorOptions{
443: 		Header: text.Colors{text.Bold},
444: 	}
445: 
446: 	headerBase := tableWriter.Row{"Name", "Kind/Image", "State", "IPv4/6 Address"}
447: 	if o.Inspect.Wide {
448: 		headerBase = slices.Insert(headerBase, 0, "Owner")
449: 	}
450: 
451: 	var header tableWriter.Row
452: 
453: 	var colConfigs []tableWriter.ColumnConfig

456: 	if o.Destroy.All {
457: 		header = append(tableWriter.Row{"Topology", "Lab Name"}, headerBase...)
458: 		colConfigs = append(
459: 			colConfigs,

465: 				Number:    2, //nolint: mnd
466: 				AutoMerge: true, VAlign: text.VAlignMiddle,
467: 			},
468: 		)

470: 			colConfigs = append(colConfigs, tableWriter.ColumnConfig{
471: 				Number:    3, //nolint: mnd
472: 				AutoMerge: true, VAlign: text.VAlignMiddle,
473: 			})
474: 		}
475: 	} else {
476: 		header = headerBase
477: 
478: 		colConfigs = append(colConfigs, tableWriter.ColumnConfig{
479: 			Number:    1,
480: 			AutoMerge: true, VAlign: text.VAlignMiddle,
481: 		})

485: 
486: 	if len(colConfigs) > 0 {
487: 		table.SetColumnConfigs(colConfigs)

490: 	table.AppendRows(tabData)

492: }

494: func printContainerInspectCSV(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails) {
495: 	csv := "lab_name,labPath,absLabPath,name,container_id,image,kind,state," +
496: 		"status,ipv4_address,ipv6_address,owner\n"
497: 	for idx := range contDetails {
498: 		csv += fmt.Sprintf("%v,%v,%v,%v,%v,%v,%v,%v,%v,%v,%v,%v\n",
499: 			contDetails[idx].LabName,
500: 			contDetails[idx].LabPath,
501: 			contDetails[idx].AbsLabPath,
502: 			contDetails[idx].Name,
503: 			contDetails[idx].ContainerID,
504: 			contDetails[idx].Image,
505: 			contDetails[idx].Kind,
506: 			contDetails[idx].State,
507: 			contDetails[idx].Status,
508: 			contDetails[idx].IPv4Address,
509: 			contDetails[idx].IPv6Address,
510: 			contDetails[idx].Owner)
511: 	}
512: 
513: 	fmt.Print(csv)
514: }

516: // PrintContainerInspect handles non-details output (table or grouped JSON summary).
517: func PrintContainerInspect(containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer, o *Options) error {
518: 	contDetails := make([]clabtypes.ContainerDetails, 0, len(containers))
519: 
520: 	// Gather summary details of each container
521: 	for idx := range containers {
522: 		absPath := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.TopoFile]
523: 
524: 		shortPath, err := getShortestTopologyPath(absPath)
525: 		if err != nil {
526: 			log.Warnf(
527: 				"failed to get relative topology path for container %s: %v, using raw path %q",
528: 				containers[idx].Names[0],
529: 				err,
530: 				absPath,
531: 			)
532: 
533: 			shortPath = absPath // Use raw path as fallback for display
534: 		}
535: 
536: 		status := parseStatus(containers[idx].Status)
537: 
538: 		cdet := clabtypes.ContainerDetails{
539: 			LabName:     containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab],
540: 			LabPath:     shortPath, // Relative or shortest path for table view
541: 			AbsLabPath:  absPath,   // Absolute path for JSON view
542: 			Image:       containers[idx].Image,
543: 			State:       containers[idx].State,
544: 			Status:      status,
545: 			IPv4Address: containers[idx].GetContainerIPv4(),
546: 			IPv6Address: containers[idx].GetContainerIPv6(),
547: 			ContainerID: containers[idx].ShortID,
548: 			// Only lab runtimes set the workload; it adds namespace, pod, container, node_uid,
549: 			// exposed_ports and load_balancer_address to the JSON output.
550: 			LabRuntimeWorkload: containers[idx].Workload,
551: 		}
552: 
553: 		if len(containers[idx].Names) > 0 {
554: 			cdet.Name = containers[idx].Names[0]
555: 		}
556: 
557: 		if group, ok := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.NodeGroup]; ok {
558: 			cdet.Group = group
559: 		}
560: 
561: 		if kind, ok := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.NodeKind]; ok {
562: 			cdet.Kind = kind
563: 		}
564: 
565: 		if owner, ok := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.Owner]; ok {
566: 			cdet.Owner = owner
567: 		}
568: 
569: 		contDetails = append(contDetails, cdet)
570: 	}
571: 
572: 	// Sort summary details by lab name, then container name
573: 	sort.Slice(contDetails, func(i, j int) bool {
574: 		if contDetails[i].LabName == contDetails[j].LabName {
575: 			return contDetails[i].Name < contDetails[j].Name
576: 		}
577: 
578: 		return contDetails[i].LabName < contDetails[j].LabName
579: 	})
580: 
581: 	switch o.Inspect.Format {
582: 	case clabconstants.FormatJSON:
583: 		err := printContainerInspectJSON(contDetails)
584: 		if err != nil {
585: 			return err
586: 		}
587: 	case "table":
588: 		printContainerInspectTable(contDetails, o)
589: 	case "csv":
590: 		printContainerInspectCSV(contDetails)
591: 	default:
592: 		return fmt.Errorf("internal error: unhandled format %q", o.Inspect.Format)
593: 	}
594: 
595: 	return nil
596: }
597: 
598: // parseStatus extracts a simpler status string, focusing on health states.
599: func parseStatus(status string) string {
600: 	switch {
601: 	case strings.Contains(status, "unhealthy"):
602: 		return "unhealthy"
603: 	case strings.Contains(status, "health: starting"):
604: 		return "health: starting"
605: 	case strings.Contains(status, "healthy"):
606: 		return "healthy"
607: 	default:
608: 		// Return original status if no specific health info found
609: 		return status
610: 	}
611: }
612: 
613: // ipWithoutPrefix removes the CIDR prefix length from an IP address string.
614: // Returns "N/A" if input contains "N/A".
615: // Returns original string if it doesn't contain exactly one "/".
616: func ipWithoutPrefix(ip string) string {
617: 	if strings.Contains(ip, clabconstants.NotApplicable) {
618: 		return ip
619: 	}
620: 
621: 	ipParts := strings.Split(ip, "/")
622: 	if len(ipParts) != 2 { //nolint: mnd
623: 		return ip // Return original if not in expected format "address/prefix"
624: 	}
625: 
626: 	return ipParts[0]
627: }

either a lab name (--name) or a topology file path (--topo) or the --all flag\",\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\t// Format validation (only relevant if --details is NOT used)\n\tif !o.Inspect.Details {\n\t\tswitch o.Inspect.Format {\n\t\tcase clabconstants.FormatJSON:\n\t\t\tfmt.Println(\"{}\")\n\t\tcase +\n\t\t\t\t\t\"ipv4_address,ipv6_address,owner\",\n\t\t\t)\n\t\tdefault: // Table format\n\t\t\tlog.Info(\"no containers found\")\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Handle --details (always PrintContainerInspect(containers, o)\n\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// listContainers handles listing containers based on different criteria (topology or labels).\nfunc listContainers(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tc *clabcore.CLab,\n\to *Options,\n) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error) {\n\tif c.LabRuntime != nil {\n\t\treturn c.ListLabRuntimeContainers(ctx, o.Destroy.All)\n\t}\n\n\tvar clabcore.WithListclabLabelExists())\n\t}\n\n\tcontainers, err := c.ListContainers(ctx, listOptions...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to list containers based on labels: %s\", c.Nodes[ctr.Labels[clabconstants.NodeName]]\n\t\t\t_, isComponent := c.Nodes[ctr.Labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName]]\n\n\t\t\treturn !isNode && !isComponent\n\t\t})\n\n\t\t// containers that containerlab did not create (ie. ext-container) have no lab labels.\n\t\tnodeContainers, err := c.ListNodesContainersIgnoreNotFound(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to node.\nfunc logicalNodeContainers(\n\tcontainers []clabruntime.GenericContainer,\n) []clabruntime.GenericContainer {\n\t// Node names are unique only within a lab.\n\ttype nodeKey struct{ lab, node string }\n\n\tholders := make(map[nodeKey]*clabruntime.GenericContainer)\n\thasCPMA := make(map[nodeKey]bool)\n\n\tfor idx := range containers {\n\t\tl := containers[idx].Labels\n\t\tkey := nodeKey{l[clabconstants.Containerlab], l[clabconstants.RootNodeName]}\n\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase key.node == \"\":\n\t\tcase l[clabconstants.InternalNode] == \"true\":\n\t\t\tholders[key] = &containers[idx]\n\t\tcase l[clabconstants.NodeName] == key.node+\"-a\":\n\t\t\thasCPMA[key] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tresult := make([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, 0, len(containers))\n\n\tfor idx := range containers {\n\t\tctr := containers[idx]\n\t\tname := ctr.Labels[clabconstants.NodeName]\n\t\tkey := nodeKey{\n\t\t\tctr.Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab],\n\t\t\tctr.Labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName],\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Components are named <root node>-<lowercase slot>.\n\t\tisCPM := name == key.node+\"-a\" || (name == key.node+\"-b\" && !hasCPMA[key])\n\t\tif ctr.Labels[clabconstants.ToolType] != \"\" ||\n\t\t\tctr.Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] == \"true\" || (key.node != \"\" && !isCPM) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif key.node == \"\" {\n\t\t\tkey.node = name\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif holder := holders[key]; holder != nil {\n\t\t\tctr.NetworkName = holder.NetworkName\n\t\t\tctr.NetworkSettings = holder.NetworkSettings\n\t\t\tctr.Ports = holder.Ports\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tresult = append(result, ctr)\n\t}\n\n\treturn result\n}\n\nfunc toTableData(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails, o *Options) []tableWriter.Row {\n\ttabData := make([]tableWriter.Row, 0, len(contDetails))\n\n\tfor i := range contDetails {\n\t\td := &contDetails[i]\n\t\ttabRow := tableWriter.Row{}\n\n\t\tif o.Destroy.All {\n\t\t\ttabRow = append(tabRow, d.LabPath, d.LabName)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif o.Inspect.Wide {\n\t\t\ttabRow = append(tabRow, d.Owner)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// we do not want to print status other than health in the table view\n\t\tif !strings.Contains(d.Status, \"health\") {\n\t\t\td.Status = \"\"\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\td.Status = fmt.Sprintf(\"(%s)\", d.Status)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Common fields\n\t\tif o.Inspect.Wide {\n\t\t\t// Print all fields on one line, no newlines\n\t\t\ttabRow = append(tabRow,\n\t\t\t\td.Name,\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", d.Kind, d.Image),\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", d.State, d.Status),\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\",\n\t\t\t\t\tipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv4Address),\n\t\t\t\t\tipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv6Address)))\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttabRow = append(tabRow,\n\t\t\t\td.Name,\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n%s\", d.Kind, d.Image),\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n%s\", d.State, d.Status),\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n%s\",\n\t\t\t\t\tipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv4Address),\n\t\t\t\t\tipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv6Address)))\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttabData = append(tabData, tabRow)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tabData\n}\n\n// getShortestTopologyPath calculates the relative path to the provided topology file from\n// the current working directory and returns it if it is shorted than the absolute path p.\nfunc getShortestTopologyPath(p string) (string, error) {\n\tif p == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\tif strings.Contains(p, \"://\") {\n\t\treturn p, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// get topo file path relative of the cwd\n\tcwd, err := os.Getwd()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\n\trelPath, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, p)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\n\tif len(relPath) < len(p) {\n\t\treturn relPath, nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn p, nil\n}\n\n// printContainerDetailsJSON handles the detailed JSON output of containers grouped by lab name.\nfunc printContainerDetailsJSON(containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer) error {\n\tgroupedDetails := make(map[string][]clabruntime.GenericContainer)\n\t// Sort containers first by lab name, then by container name for consistent output\n\tsort.Slice(containers, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\tlabNameI := containers[i].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]\n\t\tlabNameJ := containers[j].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]\n\n\t\tif labNameI == labNameJ {\n\t\t\t// Use the first name if available\n\t\t\tnameI := \"\"\n\t\t\tif len(containers[i].Names) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tnameI = containers[i].Names[0]\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tnameJ := \"\"\n\t\t\tif len(containers[j].Names) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tnameJ = containers[j].Names[0]\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn nameI < nameJ\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn labNameI < labNameJ\n\t})\n\n\t// Group the sorted containers\n\tfor idx := range containers {\n\t\tlabName := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]\n\t\t// Ensure labName exists, default to a placeholder if missing\n\t\t// (shouldn't happen with filters)\n\t\tif labName == \"\" {\n\t\t\tlabName = \"_unknown_lab_\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tgroupedDetails[labName] = append(groupedDetails[labName], containers[idx])\n\t}\n\n\tb, err := json.MarshalIndent(groupedDetails, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal grouped container details: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Println(string(b))\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc printContainerInspectJSON(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails) error {\n\t// Group summary results by LabName\n\t// Use a map *Options) {\n\t// Generate and render table using the summary data (which uses relative LabPath)\n\ttabData := toTableData(contDetails, o)\n\ttable := 105: func inspectFn(cobraCmd *cobra.Command, o *Options) error {
106: 	if o.Global.TopologyName == "" && o.Global.TopologyFile == "" && !o.Destroy.All {
107: 		return fmt.Errorf(
108: 			"provide either a lab name (--name) or a topology file path (--topo) or the --all flag",
109: 		)
110: 	}
111: 
112: 	// Format validation (only relevant if --details is NOT used)
113: 	if !o.Inspect.Details &&
114: 		o.Inspect.Format != clabconstants.FormatTable &&
115: 		o.Inspect.Format != clabconstants.FormatJSON &&
116: 		o.Inspect.Format != clabconstants.FormatCSV {
117: 		return fmt.Errorf(
118: 			"output format %q is not supported when --details is not used, use "+
119: 				"'table', 'json' or 'csv'",
120: 			o.Inspect.Format,
121: 		)
122: 	}
123: 	// If --details is used, the format is implicitly JSON.
124: 	if o.Inspect.Details {
125: 		o.Inspect.Format = clabconstants.FormatJSON // Force JSON format if details are requested
126: 	}
127: 
128: 	c, err := clabcore.NewContainerLab(o.ToClabOptions()...)
129: 	if err != nil {
130: 		return err
131: 	}
132: 
133: 	containers, err := listContainers(cobraCmd.Context(), c, o)
134: 	if err != nil {
135: 		return err
136: 	}
137: 
138: 	// Handle empty results
139: 	if len(containers) == 0 {
140: 		switch o.Inspect.Format {
141: 		case clabconstants.FormatJSON:
142: 			fmt.Println("{}")
143: 		case clabconstants.FormatCSV:
144: 			fmt.Println(
145: 				"lab_name,labPath,absLabPath,name,container_id,image,kind,state,status," +
146: 					"ipv4_address,ipv6_address,owner",
147: 			)
148: 		default: // Table format
149: 			log.Info("no containers found")
150: 		}
151: 
152: 		return err
153: 	}
154: 
155: 	// Handle --details (always produces grouped JSON output)
156: 	if o.Inspect.Details {
157: 		return printContainerDetailsJSON(containers)
158: 	}
159: 
160: 	// Handle non-details cases (table or grouped JSON summary)
161: 	err = PrintContainerInspect(containers, o)
162: 
163: 	return err
164: }

166: // listContainers handles listing containers based on different criteria (topology or labels).
167: func listContainers(
168: 	ctx context.Context,
169: 	c *clabcore.CLab,
170: 	o *Options,
171: ) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error) {
172: 	if c.LabRuntime != nil {
173: 		return c.ListLabRuntimeContainers(ctx, o.Destroy.All)
174: 	}
175: 
176: 	var containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer
177: 
178: 	var err error
179: 
180: 	if o.Global.TopologyFile != "" {
181: 		// List containers defined in the topology file
182: 		containers, err = c.ListNodesContainersIgnoreNotFound(ctx)
183: 		if err != nil {
184: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list containers based on topology: %s", err)
185: 		}
186: 	} else {
187: 		var listOptions []clabcore.ListOption
188: 
189: 		// List containers based on labels (--name or --all)
190: 		if o.Global.TopologyName != "" {
191: 			listOptions = append(listOptions, clabcore.WithListLabName(o.Global.TopologyName))
192: 		} else {
193: 			listOptions = append(listOptions, clabcore.WithListclabLabelExists())
194: 		}
195: 
196: 		containers, err = c.ListContainers(ctx, listOptions...)
197: 		if err != nil {
198: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list containers based on labels: %s", err)
199: 		}
200: 	}
201: 
202: 	// hide internal containers (ie. SR-SIM netns holder) only for non-all inspect.
203: 	if !o.Destroy.All {
204: 		containers = slices.DeleteFunc(containers, func(c clabruntime.GenericContainer) bool {
205: 			return c.Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true"
206: 		})
207: 	}
208: 
209: 	return containers, nil
210: }

212: func toTableData(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails, o *Options) []tableWriter.Row {
213: 	tabData := make([]tableWriter.Row, 0, len(contDetails))
214: 
215: 	for i := range contDetails {
216: 		d := &contDetails[i]
217: 		tabRow := tableWriter.Row{}
218: 
219: 		if o.Destroy.All {
220: 			tabRow = append(tabRow, d.LabPath, d.LabName)
221: 		}
222: 
223: 		if o.Inspect.Wide {
224: 			tabRow = append(tabRow, d.Owner)
225: 		}
226: 
227: 		// we do not want to print status other than health in the table view
228: 		if !strings.Contains(d.Status, "health") {
229: 			d.Status = ""
230: 		} else {
231: 			d.Status = fmt.Sprintf("(%s)", d.Status)
232: 		}
233: 
234: 		// Common fields
235: 		if o.Inspect.Wide {
236: 			// Print all fields on one line, no newlines
237: 			tabRow = append(tabRow,
238: 				d.Name,
239: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", d.Kind, d.Image),
240: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", d.State, d.Status),
241: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s %s",
242: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv4Address),
243: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv6Address)))
244: 		} else {
245: 			tabRow = append(tabRow,
246: 				d.Name,
247: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s\n%s", d.Kind, d.Image),
248: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s\n%s", d.State, d.Status),
249: 				fmt.Sprintf("%s\n%s",
250: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv4Address),
251: 					ipWithoutPrefix(d.IPv6Address)))
252: 		}
253: 
254: 		tabData = append(tabData, tabRow)
255: 	}
256: 
257: 	return tabData
258: }
259: 
260: // getShortestTopologyPath calculates the relative path to the provided topology file from
261: // the current working directory and returns it if it is shorted than the absolute path p.
262: func getShortestTopologyPath(p string) (string, error) {
263: 	if p == "" {
264: 		return "", nil
265: 	}
266: 	if strings.Contains(p, "://") {
267: 		return p, nil
268: 	}
269: 
270: 	// get topo file path relative of the cwd
271: 	cwd, err := os.Getwd()
272: 	if err != nil {
273: 		return "", err
274: 	}
275: 
276: 	relPath, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, p)
277: 	if err != nil {
278: 		return "", err
279: 	}
280: 
281: 	if len(relPath) < len(p) {
282: 		return relPath, nil
283: 	}
284: 
285: 	return p, nil
286: }
287: 
288: // printContainerDetailsJSON handles the detailed JSON output of containers grouped by lab name.
289: func printContainerDetailsJSON(containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer) error {
290: 	groupedDetails := make(map[string][]clabruntime.GenericContainer)
291: 	// Sort containers first by lab name, then by container name for consistent output
292: 	sort.Slice(containers, func(i, j int) bool {
293: 		labNameI := containers[i].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]
294: 		labNameJ := containers[j].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]
295: 
296: 		if labNameI == labNameJ {
297: 			// Use the first name if available
298: 			nameI := ""
299: 			if len(containers[i].Names) > 0 {
300: 				nameI = containers[i].Names[0]
301: 			}
302: 
303: 			nameJ := ""
304: 			if len(containers[j].Names) > 0 {
305: 				nameJ = containers[j].Names[0]
306: 			}
307: 
308: 			return nameI < nameJ
309: 		}
310: 
311: 		return labNameI < labNameJ
312: 	})
313: 
314: 	// Group the sorted containers
315: 	for idx := range containers {
316: 		labName := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab]
317: 		// Ensure labName exists, default to a placeholder if missing
318: 		// (shouldn't happen with filters)
319: 		if labName == "" {
320: 			labName = "_unknown_lab_"
321: 		}
322: 
323: 		groupedDetails[labName] = append(groupedDetails[labName], containers[idx])
324: 	}
325: 
326: 	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(groupedDetails, "", "  ")
327: 	if err != nil {
328: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal grouped container details: %v", err)
329: 	}
330: 
331: 	fmt.Println(string(b))
332: 
333: 	return nil
334: }
335: 
336: func printContainerInspectJSON(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails) error {
337: 	// Group summary results by LabName
338: 	// Use a map where keys are lab names and values are slices of container details
339: 	groupedLabs := make(map[string][]clabtypes.ContainerDetails)
340: 
341: 	for idx := range contDetails {
342: 		labName := contDetails[idx].LabName
343: 		if labName == "" {
344: 			labName = "_unknown_lab_" // Should not happen if filters work correctly
345: 		}
346: 		// Assign the *entire* ContainerDetails struct (including AbsLabPath)
347: 		groupedLabs[labName] = append(groupedLabs[labName], contDetails[idx])
348: 	}
349: 
350: 	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(groupedLabs, "", "  ")
351: 	if err != nil {
352: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal grouped container summary: %v", err)
353: 	}
354: 
355: 	fmt.Println(string(b))
356: 
357: 	return nil
358: }

360: func printContainerInspectTable(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails, o *Options) {
361: 	// Generate and render table using the summary data (which uses relative LabPath)
362: 	tabData := toTableData(contDetails, o)
363: 	table := tableWriter.NewWriter()
364: 	table.SetOutputMirror(os.Stdout)
365: 	table.SetStyle(tableWriter.StyleRounded)
366: 	table.Style().Format.Header = text.FormatTitle
367: 	table.Style().Format.HeaderAlign = text.AlignCenter
368: 	table.Style().Options.SeparateRows = true
369: 	table.Style().Color = tableWriter.ColorOptions{
370: 		Header: text.Colors{text.Bold},
371: 	}
372: 
373: 	headerBase := tableWriter.Row{"Name", "Kind/Image", "State", "IPv4/6 Address"}
374: 	if o.Inspect.Wide {
375: 		headerBase = slices.Insert(headerBase, 0, "Owner")
376: 	}
377: 
378: 	var header tableWriter.Row
379: 
380: 	var colConfigs []tableWriter.ColumnConfig
381: 
382: 	// Lab-level columns (Topology, Lab Name, Owner) AutoMerge across nodes of the same lab.
383: 	if o.Destroy.All {
384: 		header = append(tableWriter.Row{"Topology", "Lab Name"}, headerBase...)
385: 		colConfigs = append(
386: 			colConfigs,
387: 			tableWriter.ColumnConfig{
388: 				Number:    1,
389: 				AutoMerge: true, VAlign: text.VAlignMiddle,
390: 			},
391: 			tableWriter.ColumnConfig{
392: 				Number:    2, //nolint: mnd
393: 				AutoMerge: true, VAlign: text.VAlignMiddle,
394: 			},
395: 		)
396: 		if o.Inspect.Wide {
397: 			colConfigs = append(colConfigs, tableWriter.ColumnConfig{
398: 				Number:    3, //nolint: mnd
399: 				AutoMerge: true, VAlign: text.VAlignMiddle,
400: 			})
401: 		}
402: 	} else {
403: 		header = headerBase
404: 
405: 		colConfigs = append(colConfigs, tableWriter.ColumnConfig{
406: 			Number:    1,
407: 			AutoMerge: true, VAlign: text.VAlignMiddle,
408: 		})
409: 	}
410: 
411: 	table.AppendHeader(header)
412: 
413: 	if len(colConfigs) > 0 {
414: 		table.SetColumnConfigs(colConfigs)
415: 	}
416: 
417: 	table.AppendRows(tabData)
418: 	table.Render()
419: }

421: func printContainerInspectCSV(contDetails []clabtypes.ContainerDetails) {
422: 	csv := "lab_name,labPath,absLabPath,name,container_id,image,kind,state," +
423: 		"status,ipv4_address,ipv6_address,owner\n"
424: 	for idx := range contDetails {

426: 			contDetails[idx].LabName,
427: 			contDetails[idx].LabPath,
428: 			contDetails[idx].AbsLabPath,

430: 			contDetails[idx].ContainerID,
431: 			contDetails[idx].Image,
432: 			contDetails[idx].Kind,
433: 			contDetails[idx].State,
434: 			contDetails[idx].Status,
435: 			contDetails[idx].IPv4Address,
436: 			contDetails[idx].IPv6Address,
437: 			contDetails[idx].Owner)
438: 	}

441: }

443: // PrintContainerInspect handles non-details output (table or grouped JSON summary).
444: func PrintContainerInspect(containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer, o *Options) error {
445: 	contDetails := make([]clabtypes.ContainerDetails, 0, len(containers))
446: 
447: 	// Gather summary details of each container
448: 	for idx := range containers {
449: 		if !o.Destroy.All && containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true" {
450: 			continue
451: 		}
452: 		absPath := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.TopoFile]
453: 
454: 		shortPath, err := getShortestTopologyPath(absPath)
455: 		if err != nil {
456: 			log.Warnf(
457: 				"failed to get relative topology path for container %s: %v, using raw path %q",
458: 				containers[idx].Names[0],
459: 				err,
460: 				absPath,
461: 			)
462: 
463: 			shortPath = absPath // Use raw path as fallback for display
464: 		}
465: 
466: 		status := parseStatus(containers[idx].Status)
467: 
468: 		cdet := clabtypes.ContainerDetails{
469: 			LabName:     containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.Containerlab],
470: 			LabPath:     shortPath, // Relative or shortest path for table view
471: 			AbsLabPath:  absPath,   // Absolute path for JSON view
472: 			Image:       containers[idx].Image,
473: 			State:       containers[idx].State,
474: 			Status:      status,
475: 			IPv4Address: containers[idx].GetContainerIPv4(),
476: 			IPv6Address: containers[idx].GetContainerIPv6(),
477: 			ContainerID: containers[idx].ShortID,
478: 			// Only lab runtimes set the workload; it adds namespace, pod, container, node_uid,
479: 			// exposed_ports and load_balancer_address to the JSON output.
480: 			LabRuntimeWorkload: containers[idx].Workload,
481: 		}
482: 
483: 		if len(containers[idx].Names) > 0 {
484: 			cdet.Name = containers[idx].Names[0]
485: 		}
486: 
487: 		if group, ok := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.NodeGroup]; ok {
488: 			cdet.Group = group
489: 		}
490: 
491: 		if kind, ok := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.NodeKind]; ok {
492: 			cdet.Kind = kind
493: 		}
494: 
495: 		if owner, ok := containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.Owner]; ok {
496: 			cdet.Owner = owner
497: 		}
498: 
499: 		contDetails = append(contDetails, cdet)
500: 	}
501: 
502: 	// Sort summary details by lab name, then container name
503: 	sort.Slice(contDetails, func(i, j int) bool {
504: 		if contDetails[i].LabName == contDetails[j].LabName {
505: 			return contDetails[i].Name < contDetails[j].Name
506: 		}
507: 
508: 		return contDetails[i].LabName < contDetails[j].LabName
509: 	})
510: 
511: 	switch o.Inspect.Format {
512: 	case clabconstants.FormatJSON:
513: 		err := printContainerInspectJSON(contDetails)
514: 		if err != nil {
515: 			return err
516: 		}
517: 	case "table":
518: 		printContainerInspectTable(contDetails, o)
519: 	case "csv":
520: 		printContainerInspectCSV(contDetails)
521: 	default:
522: 		return fmt.Errorf("internal error: unhandled format %q", o.Inspect.Format)
523: 	}
524: 
525: 	return nil
526: }
527: 
528: // parseStatus extracts a simpler status string, focusing on health states.
529: func parseStatus(status string) string {
530: 	switch {
531: 	case strings.Contains(status, "unhealthy"):
532: 		return "unhealthy"
533: 	case strings.Contains(status, "health: starting"):
534: 		return "health: starting"
535: 	case strings.Contains(status, "healthy"):
536: 		return "healthy"
537: 	default:
538: 		// Return original status if no specific health info found
539: 		return status
540: 	}
541: }
542: 
543: // ipWithoutPrefix removes the CIDR prefix length from an IP address string.
544: // Returns "N/A" if input contains "N/A".
545: // Returns original string if it doesn't contain exactly one "/".
546: func ipWithoutPrefix(ip string) string {
547: 	if strings.Contains(ip, clabconstants.NotApplicable) {
548: 		return ip
549: 	}
550: 
551: 	ipParts := strings.Split(ip, "/")
552: 	if len(ipParts) != 2 { //nolint: mnd
553: 		return ip // Return original if not in expected format "address/prefix"
554: 	}
555: 
556: 	return ipParts[0]
557: }

Reasoning
Now I understand the change. Let me trace the semantics of `logicalNodeContainers`:

Key labels:
- `clabconstants.Containerlab` = "containerlab" (lab name)
- `NodeName` = "clab-node-name"
- `RootNodeName` = "clab-root-node-name" (for components of a multi-container node)
- `InternalNode` = "clab-internal-node"
- `ToolType` = "tool-type"

The logic:
1. First pass: for each container, key = (lab, rootNodeName).
   - if rootNodeName empty: skip.
   - if internal node: holders[key] = &containers[idx] — note: this takes a pointer to slice element; last internal holder wins.
   - if NodeName == rootNodeName+"-a": hasCPMA[key] = true.
2. Second pass: for each container:
   - name = NodeName
   - key = (lab, rootNodeName)
   - isCPM := name == key.node+"-a" || (name == key.node+"-b" && !hasCPMA[key])
   - skip if ToolType != "" || InternalNode=="true" || (key.node != "" && !isCPM)
   - if key.node == "": key.node = name
   - copy holder network info into ctr (ctr is a copy — but note ctr.NetworkSettings is set from holder; but wait, `ctr := containers[idx]` makes a copy; then `ctr.NetworkName = holder.NetworkName` etc. Then append. OK.)
   - append.

Wait — there's a subtlety: for the logical node, the code sets `ctr.NetworkSettings = holder.NetworkSettings`. But for the srl node ("srl" with empty ipv4 in the test), holder "srl-netns" has ipv4 "10.0.0.2". The logical node carries holder's address. Good.

But what about a node that has its own IP (linux, 10.0.0.1)? holders key = ("lab1", "") — key.node == "" means skipped in first pass. So no holder. Fine.

Now potential issues:

1. **The `holders` map stores pointers into the slice `containers`** — `&containers[idx]`. That's fine within this function (no reslicing/appending to containers that reallocates; DeleteFunc happened earlier). OK.

2. **hasCPMA detection**: components are named `<root node>-<lowercase slot>`. CPM A is `sr-a`. But what about a node actually named `x-a`? E.g., a plain node named "sr" with no components: NodeName="sr", RootNodeName="" → key.node=="" → in second pass, isCPM irrelevant since key.node=="" means the `(key.node != "" && !isCPM)` is false → not skipped. Fine.

   But consider a component node where RootNodeName is set: e.g. `sr` with components. NodeName = "sr-a", RootNodeName = "sr". key.node="sr". isCPM: name == "sr-a" → true. Good. For slot "1": name = "sr-1" → isCPM false → skipped. Good.

   However, what if a user names a regular (non-component) node ending with `-a`? e.g., node "neta"? Then RootNodeName is "" so key.node == "" → included as logical node. Fine.

   What about a lab where a plain node is named such that its name equals `<root>-b`? Not applicable since RootNodeName empty.

3. **Interaction between labs: `hasCPMA` keyed by (lab, node)** — OK.

4. **Edge case: SR Linux node ("srl") with internal netns holder "srl-netns" whose RootNodeName="srl".** In second pass, "srl" itself: NodeName="srl", RootNodeName="" → key.node="" → included; holders key ("lab1","") — holder for "srl-netns" is stored under key ("lab1","srl"), not ("lab1",""). So ctr.NetworkSettings stays as the srl container's own (which is empty in test, `""`). Wait the test expects `clab-lab1-srl 10.0.0.2` — the logical node carries the holder's address. Hmm, but holder is keyed ("lab1","srl") and srl's own key is ("lab1",""). So the holder lookup for the logical "srl" node uses key ("lab1","") which has no holder... 

   Wait, let me re-read. In the second pass for container "srl" (NodeName="srl", RootNodeName absent):
   - key = {"lab1", ""}
   - name = "srl"
   - isCPM = (name == ""+"-a")? No... key.node is "" so `name == key.node+"-a"` → name == "-a" → false. And `name == key.node+"-b"` → "srl"=="-b" false. So isCPM = false.
   - skip condition: ToolType != "" (false) || InternalNode=="true" (false) || (key.node != "" && !isCPM) — key.node=="" so third clause false → not skipped.
   - key.node == "" → key.node = name = "srl". So now key = {"lab1", "srl"}.
   - holder lookup holders[{"lab1","srl"}] = the "srl-netns" container → copies NetworkSettings (10.0.0.2). 

   So the holder is stored under ("lab1","srl") because the holder's RootNodeName="srl". And the logical node "srl" gets remapped. Clever. Good.

5. **The `-a`/`--all` behavior change**: Base: `if !o.Destroy.All { hide internal }`. Head: internal containers are always hidden unless `--containers` or `--details`. So `inspect --all` previously showed internal containers; now it hides them unless `--containers`. 17: // usesNetnsNode reports whether the node runs in the network namespace of a holder container.
18: // Nodes with a user-defined network mode keep the network namespace of their own container.
19: func (n *srl) usesNetnsNode() bool {
20: 	return n.Cfg.NetworkMode == "" && !n.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased
21: }
22: 
23: func (n *srl) initNetnsNode() error {
24: 	if !n.usesNetnsNode() {
25: 		return nil
26: 	}

91: // containerConfig returns the config of the SR Linux container attached to the holder's
92: // network namespace. The runtime rejects network settings for such containers, as they
93: // belong to the holder.
94: func (n *srl) containerConfig() *clabtypes.NodeConfig {
95: 	cfg := *n.Cfg
96: 	cfg.NetworkMode = "container:" + n.netnsNode.GetShortName()
97: 	cfg.DNS = nil
98: 	cfg.ExtraHosts = nil
99: 	cfg.MacAddress = ""
100: 	cfg.PortBindings = nil

105: 
106: // linkNetns points the node's netns symlink to the holder, as the namespace path of the
107: // SR Linux container changes whenever the container restarts.
108: func (n *srl) linkNetns(ctx context.Context) error {
109: 	nsPath, err := n.netnsNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
110: 	if err != nil {
111: 		return err
112: 	}
113: 
114: 	return clabutils.LinkContainerNS(nsPath, n.Cfg.LongName)
115: }
116: 
117: // deployedWithoutNetnsNode reports whether the node's container runs without a holder: it keeps
118: // its own network namespace, as a node deployed by a containerlab before the holder does until
119: // it is recreated.
120: func (n *srl) deployedWithoutNetnsNode(ctx context.Context) bool {
121: 	return n.netnsNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx) == clabruntime.NotFound &&
122: 		n.GetContainerStatus(ctx) != clabruntime.NotFound
123: }
124: 
125: // GetNSPath retrieves the Namespace Path.
126: func (n *srl) GetNSPath(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
127: 	if n.netnsNode == nil || n.deployedWithoutNetnsNode(ctx) {
128: 		return n.DefaultNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
129: 	}
130: 
131: 	return n.netnsNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
132: }
133: 
134: // DeleteNetnsSymlink deletes the symlink file created for the container netns.
135: func (n *srl) DeleteNetnsSymlink() error {
136: 	if n.netnsNode == nil {
137: 		return n.DefaultNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink()
138: 	}
139: 
140: 	return errors.Join(n.DefaultNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink(), n.netnsNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink())

842: 	if n.renameDone {
843: 		return n.Cfg.Fqdn
844: 	}
845: 	fqdnDotIndex := strings.Index(n.Cfg.Fqdn, ".")

Reasoning
Key insight: `cpmSlot()` errors if no slot A and no slot B among components. So for a distributed SR-SIM node, components must include A or B (a CPM is mandatory for distributed). So hasCPMA false with CPM-B only is the only fallback case. So `isCPM` heuristic seems consistent with `cpmSlot()`: prefer A, fall back to B. Good — mirrors the existing helper's semantics.

Reasoning
For integrated redundant SR-SIM (e.g., node type sr-1 with components A and B): `isDistributedBaseNode` returns true → `deployFabric` is used: deploys netns holder + component containers, and the base node container is NOT deployed (Deploy returns after deployFabric). So the base container doesn't exist. Good — so for integrated redundant, containers are: sr-1-a, sr-1-b (components), sr-1-netns (holder). Logical view: sr-1-a only (hasCPMA true). That matches the srsim distributed test where sr14s node yields one row.

Hmm wait, but for integrated redundant, are slots beyond A/B allowed? `integratedSrosSlotAllowed` — allowed slots per node type. If integrated node type allows slots like "1", "2" too (e.g. chassis with CPMs and line cards)? Then containers sr-1-1, etc., exist and are skipped in logical view. Fine.

Now let's think about the `hasCPMA` detection subtlety: it's keyed on `NodeName == key.node+"-a"`. But `key.node` here is the RootNodeName label of the component, which equals `n.Cfg.ShortName` of the root. And component short name = root + "-" + lowercased slot. Good.

**Critical check: what if a topology has BOTH a node named `x-a` (plain node) and a distributed node `x`?** For lab1: node "x-a" plain: NodeName=x-a, RootNodeName="" → key ("lab1",""), not skipped, appended as its own logical entry. Node "x" distributed: components x-a (RootNodeName=x) → hasCPMA [("lab1","x")]=true. That's fine — hasCPMA map only used for entries with key.node set. No interference. Good.

**Another edge: two labs, both with node named "sr"** — keys include lab name. Good (test covers).

**Edge: srl node named "srl" whose holder label RootNodeName="srl"; ALSO a component-based srsim node named "srl"? Not possible in same lab (unique node names).**

Now the important question about **`--topo` filter using `c.Nodes`**: `c.Nodes[ctr.Labels[NodeName]]` — for tool containers (sshx, gotty, vxlan, etc.), what is their NodeName label? Let me check tools node labels. If tools have NodeName set (e.g. "sshx"), then with `--topo` they'd be filtered out (not in c.Nodes). But previously (base), with `--topo`, `ListNodesContainersIgnoreNotFound` — did it include tool containers? Tools are not topology nodes, so probably not. But wait — actually, is `c.Nodes` including tool containers? Let me check. Actually more important: does `ListNodesContainersIgnoreNotFound` include ext-containers and tool containers? Let me look at that function.
Let me examine `ListNodesContainersIgnoreNotFound` and `ListContainers` in core, plus how tool containers get labels.
